Use when writing or reviewing a launchd plist and script pair, or a cron job, that does git pull, generate, commit, push from a long-lived clone, or when auditing set -euo pipefail automation for what the NEXT… ([abhijitbansal/claude-skills](https://github.com/abhijitbansal/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/core-workflow/skills/launchd-git-automation-self-heal) · ★1)
- **launchd-log-rotation-fd-rebind**: Rotate a LaunchAgent log file from within its own wrapper script without losing post-rotation log output. ([Dbochman/dotfiles](https://github.com/Dbochman/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/launchd-log-rotation-fd-rebind) · ★2)
- **launchd-non-apple-jobs**: List launchd jobs that are not from Apple and create user launchd jobs. Use when the user says "show me my jobs", "show my launchd jobs", asks for non-Apple launchd jobs, or wants to create a one-time or recurring launchd job. ([IvanCampos/agents](https://github.com/IvanCampos/agents/tree/HEAD/skills/launchd-non-apple-jobs) · ★5)
- **launchd-plist-template**: Use when installing/modifying any ~/Library/LaunchAgents plist. Requires template in repo that owns the job; @HOME@ placeholders; install-launchagents.sh cleanup. ([jleechanorg/jleechan-skills](https://github.com/jleechanorg/jleechan-skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/launchd-plist-template) · ★6)
- **launchd-process-health-check**: When checking `launchctl list | grep &lt;name&gt;`, the Exit code and PID columns can be **misleading**. A process can: - Show "Exit -15" but actually be running (someone else restarted it) - Show old PID but have been replaced by a new process - Be managed by launchd but running under a different binary ([humanerd-drew/opencode-drewgent](https://github.com/humanerd-drew/opencode-drewgent/tree/HEAD/skills/brain/launchd-process-health-check) · ★3)
